%o A058349 (Python)
%o A058349 def A058349(n: int) -> int:
%o A058349     """ Also defined for n = 0 and returns 0 in that case. """
%o A058349     ret, fact = 0, 1
%o A058349     s = -1 if (n - 1) % 2 else 1
%o A058349     for i in range(n):
%o A058349         S, c = 0, 1
%o A058349         b = i + n - 1
%o A058349         for k in range(2*i + 1):
%o A058349             d = k - i
%o A058349             if d == 0: p = 0 if b > 0 else 1
%o A058349             else: p = d ** b
%o A058349             S += c * p * (s * (-1 if d % 2 else 1))
%o A058349             if k < 2*i: c = c * (2*i - k) // (k + 1)
%o A058349         fact = fact * i if i > 0 else 1
%o A058349         ret += S // fact
%o A058349     return ret

%o A091593 (SageMath) # Algorithm of L. Seidel (1877)
%o A091593 def A091593_list(n) :
%o A091593     D = [0]*(n+2); D[1] = 1
%o A091593     R = []; b = false; h = 1
%o A091593     for i in range(2*n) :
%o A091593         if b :
%o A091593             for k in range(1, h, 1) : D[k] += -2*D[k+1]
%o A091593             R.append(D[1])
%o A091593         else :
%o A091593             for k in range(h, 0, -1) : D[k] += D[k-1]
%o A091593             h += 1
%o A091593         b = not b
%o A091593     return R

%o A239903 function CatalanNumerals(z)
%o A239903     z == 0 && return 0
%o A239903     f(n) = factorial(n)
%o A239903     t(j, k) = div(f(k+j)*(k-j+1), f(j)*f(k+1))
%o A239903     k, i = 2, 0
%o A239903     while z >= t(i, i + 1) i += 1 end
%o A239903     dig = fill(0, i); dig[1] = 1
%o A239903     x = z - t(i - 1, i)
%o A239903     m = i - 1
%o A239903     while x > 0
%o A239903         w, s, p = 0, 0, 0
%o A239903         while w <= x
%o A239903             p = w
%o A239903             w += t(m - 1, m + s)
%o A239903             s += 1
%o A239903         end
%o A239903         dig[k] = s - 1
%o A239903         m -= 1; k += 1; x -= p
%o A239903     end
%o A239903     s = ""; for d in dig s *= string(d) end
%o A239903     parse(Int, s)
%o A239903 end

%o A368482 S : makelist (0, Len)$
%o A368482 G : makelist (0, Len)$ DegG : makelist (0, Len)$   /* G, the numerator of s() */

%o A368482       SS : SS + S[i - j] * S[i - N + j]
%o A368482    ),

%o A368482    /* for N > 3 G is a homogenous polynomial, take the first monomial to determine the degree */

%o A000045 function fibrec(n::Int)
%o A000045     n == 0 && return (BigInt(0), BigInt(1))
%o A000045     a, b = fibrec(div(n, 2))
%o A000045     c = a * (b * 2 - a)
%o A000045     d = a * a + b * b
%o A000045     iseven(n) ? (c, d) : (d, c + d)
%o A000045 end

%o A000111 def A000111_list(n) :
%o A000111     R = []; A = {-1:0, 0:1}; k = 0; e = 1
%o A000111     for i in (0..n) :
%o A000111         Am = 0; A[k + e] = 0; e = -e
%o A000111         for j in (0..i) : Am += A[k]; A[k] = Am; k += e

%o A000041 ;; For k outside the range (1-(sqrt(1-24n))/6 to (1+sqrt(1-24n))/6) argument n-k(3k-1)/2 < 0.
%o A000041 ;; Therefore the loops below are finite. The hash avoids repeated identical computations.
%o A000041 (define (p n) ; Nr of partitions of n.
%o A000041 (hash-ref h n
%o A000041   (λ ()
%o A000041    (define r
%o A000041     (+
%o A000041      (let loop ((k 1) (n (sub1 n)) (s 0))
%o A000041       (if (< n 0) s
%o A000041        (loop (add1 k) (- n (* 3 k) 1) (if (odd? k) (+ s (p n)) (- s (p n))))))
%o A000041      (let loop ((k -1) (n (- n 2)) (s 0))
%o A000041       (if (< n 0) s
%o A000041        (loop (sub1 k) (+ n (* 3 k) -2) (if (odd? k) (+ s (p n)) (- s (p n))))))))
%o A000041    (hash-set! h n r)
%o A000041    r)))

%o A000364 (Python)  # after _Mikhail Kurkov_, based on an algorithm of _Peter Bala_, cf. link in A110501.
%o A000364 def euler_list(len: int) -> list[int]:
%o A000364     if len == 0: return []
%o A000364     v1 = [1] + [0] * (len - 1)
%o A000364     v2 = [i**2 for i in range(1, len + 1)]
%o A000364     result = [0] * len
%o A000364     result[0] = 1
%o A000364     for i in range(1, len):
%o A000364         for j in range(1, i):
%o A000364             v1[j] += v2[i - j] * v1[j - 1]
%o A000364         v1[i] = v1[i - 1]
%o A000364         result[i] = v1[i]
%o A000364     return result
%o A000364 print(euler_list(1000))  # _Peter Luschny_, Aug 30 2025

%o A013661 (Python)  # Use some guard digits when computing.
%o A013661 # BBP formula (3 / 16) P(2, 64, 6, (16, -24, -8, -6,  1, 0)).
%o A013661 from decimal import Decimal as dec, getcontext
%o A013661 def BBPzeta2(n: int) -> dec:
%o A013661     getcontext().prec = n
%o A013661     s = dec(0); f = dec(1); g = dec(64)
%o A013661     for k in range(int(n * 0.5536546824812272) + 1):
%o A013661         sixk = dec(6 * k)
%o A013661         s += f * ( dec(16) / (sixk + 1) ** 2 - dec(24) / (sixk + 2) ** 2
%o A013661                  - dec(8)  / (sixk + 3) ** 2 - dec(6)  / (sixk + 4) ** 2
%o A013661                  + dec(1)  / (sixk + 5) ** 2 )
%o A013661         f /= g
%o A013661     return (s * dec(3)) / dec(16)
%o A013661 print(BBPzeta2(2000))  # _Peter Luschny_, Nov 01 2023
